Key sequence motifs include:<br>- &quot;VCCGTADWT&quot; — resembles a SAM-binding motif common in methyltransferases<br>- &quot;DFSENMLSVGEQKVKDGG&quot; — part of the catalytic domain<br>- &quot;VVLETSQPEMFGFR&quot; — catalytic pocket residues<br><br>**Step 2: Identify the protein**<br>This sequence is essentially identical to *E. coli* **UbiG** (UniProt P0A1S7), with only minor differences in the N-terminal region suggesting a closely related organism.
